<div aria-labelledby="myModalLabel" class="modal fade" id="steam-game-server-login-token-modal" role="dialog" tabindex="-1">
<div class="modal-dialog modal-lg" role="document">
<div class="modal-content">
<div class="modal-header">
<h3 class="modal-title" id="myModalLabel">Add a token for your server</h3>
</div>
<div class="modal-body" style="text-align: left;">
<p>
                Valve recently made a change to how their CS:GO servers operate. To start your CS:GO server, you’ll now need a Gameserver Login Token (GSLT) to be registered with your server.
            </p>
<h4 style="color: #000">Step 1 - Go to the GSLT creation page</h4>
<p>
                You can create a token for your game servers by going to Valve’s official steam gameserver login token page here:<br/>
<a href="https://steamcommunity.com/login/home/?goto=dev%2Fmanagegameservers" target="_blank">http://steamcommunity.com/dev/managegameservers</a>
</p>
<h4 style="color: #000">Step 2 - Create your token</h4>
<p>
                To create a token you’ll need to fill in the two fields at the bottom of the page under “Create a new game server account”:<br/>
<img src="https://cdn.dathost.net/assets/img/steam-token-1.png" style="width: 100%;"/><br/>
<b>App ID:</b> Fill in the ID of the game you want to create a token for. In this case, 730 for CS:GO.<br/>
<b>Memo:</b> Fill in the name of your new token. Make sure it is something you remember so you know which server this is for. For example, it could be the same as the hostname of your server.
            </p>
<h4 style="color: #000">Step 3 - Get the ID of your newly generated token</h4>
<p>
<img src="https://cdn.dathost.net/assets/img/steam-token-2.png" style="width: 100%;"/><br/>
                Once you click create, your “Login Token” will appear. Simply copy the string and paste it in the input field below:
                </p><form class="form" id="steam-game-server-login-token-modal-form">
<input class="form-control" id="steam-game-server-login-token" pattern="^ *[a-fA-F0-9]{32} *$" required="" style="width:100%; border: 1px solid #aaa;" title="Follow the instructions above to add a valid GameServer Login Token (required by Valve)." type="text"/><br/>
<input class="btn btn-primary" id="steam-game-server-login-token-submit" style="width:100%;" type="submit" value="Add Token"/>
<div style="position: absolute; width: 100%; text-align: center;">
<img id="steam-game-server-login-token-spinner" src="https://cdn.dathost.net/assets/img/spinner.gif" style="margin-top: 7px; position: relative; left: -15px; display: none;"/>
</div>
</form>
<br/>
<i>You can also update your token any time under "Edit".</i>
<p></p>
</div>
</div>

<script>
        function steamGameServerLoginTokenModal(submitValue, callback) {
            $("#steam-game-server-login-token-submit").attr('value', submitValue);
            $("#steam-game-server-login-token-modal-form").on('submit', function (e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                $("#steam-game-server-login-token-modal-form").off('submit');
                $("#steam-game-server-login-token-spinner").show();
                callback($("#steam-game-server-login-token").val());
                $("#steam-game-server-login-token-modal").modal('hide');
            });
            $("#steam-game-server-login-token-modal").on('shown.bs.modal', function () {
                $("#steam-game-server-login-token").focus();
            });
            $("#steam-game-server-login-token-modal").modal({backdrop: 'static', keyboard: false});
        }
    </script>
